This algorithm is a coordinate alternate:
- Given a: compute P=|A|^2, φ=angle(A), θ=2φ.
- Optimize P (amplitude of A^2) to reduce q for fixed θ. This directly minimizes convolution peak while keeping a's Fourier phase unchanged. Since q depends only on P and θ, this is a convex subproblem. Great.
- Then update A amplitude from P; get a_candidate; enforce nonnegativity by soft clipping; then recompute P/φ.
This may converge well and more principled than previous.

We can also optimize phase instead of amplitude? Changing φ while P fixed changes q phases, may reduce peaks too. But we can let a clipping adjust phase. Or we can do small random phase perturbations.

Outer loop P-optimizer:
```
def amp_optimize(a, max_outer=3, sub_iters=200, beta_y=1000, beta_a=?)
  a = normalize(a)
  current score
  for outer:
    A = rfft(a,F); P=|A|^2; angle=angle(A)
    P[0]=1; P=np.clip(P,0,1)
    x0=P[1:]
    res = minimize(p_objective, x0, args=(phase, F, M, n, beta_y, 2n), bounds=, maxiter=sub_iters)
    P_new = [1]+res.x clipped 0,1
    A_new = sqrt(P_new) * exp(1j angle)
    a_new = irfft(A_new,F)[:n]
    # candidates: normalize, clip, blends
    update best a if score improved
    # optional: if no improvement, break or reduce sub_iters
  return best
```

def fast_conv(a):

   F=2*n*L/(S*S)

   # gradient L wrt a:
   Fft=next_pow2(2*n-1) # same

   r=np.fft.irfft(W*np.conj(A), Fft)[:n]
   grad_L = 2*r
   grad_F = (2*n)/(S*S) * (grad_L - (2*L/S)*np.ones(n))

```

Propose_candidate pseudo:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
  start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+budget_s-60
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  best_a = None; best_score=np.inf
  # define helper update? cannot closure. We'll manual.
  parent = load...
  if parent: best=score(parent); best_a=parent
  # quick parent previous fienup_v2 maybe top-level function returns; update
  # Build candidate list lengths and inits
  for n in lengths:
    if n too large or time out break
    inits = get_inits(n, parent, rng)
    for each init:
       if time out break
       allocate time per candidate e.g. 60-300s
       score_init = compute_score(init)
       # run amp_phase_optimizer with deadline subdeadline
       a_opt, sc_opt = amp_phase_optimizer(init, deadline=..., label=...)
       if sc_opt < best_score: update
       # optionally run a_lbfgs polish from a_opt
  # final polish best with a_lbfgs using remaining time
  return list(best_a)
```
Need not use closures. We can write small top-level `try_update(score, a, current_best)`? But returning tuple. Manual.

Let's detail `a_lbfgs_objective`:
```
def a_lbfgs_objective(x, beta_y):
    n = len(x)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    # since bounds nonneg, but safe clip
    if x.sum() <= 1e-12:
       return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    c = fast_convolution(x)
    C = float(np.max(c))
    if C <= 1e-30 or not np.isfinite(C): return 1e100, zeros
    # scale
    scaled = (beta_y * (c - C)) / C
    scaled = np.clip(scaled, -745.0, 0.0)
    exps = np.exp(scaled)
    z = float(np.sum(exps))
    if z <= 0:
       exps[0]=1.0; z=1.0
    L = C + C * np.log(z) / beta_y
    S = float(np.sum(x))
    F = (2.0 * n * L) / (S*S)
    weights = exps / z
    Ff = fft_size(n)
    W = np.fft.rfft(weights, Ff)
    A = np.fft.rfft(x, Ff)
    r = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(A), Ff)[:n]
    grad_L = 2.0 * r
    grad_F = (2.0*n)/(S*S) * (grad_L - (2.0*L/S) * np.ones(n))
    # replace nan/inf
    if not np.isfinite(F): return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    grad_F = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_F), grad_F, 0.0)
    return float(F), grad_F
```
Check `np.fft.rfft(weights, Ff)` with weights length M=Ff maybe if Ff==M? We set Ff=nextpow2(M), so if M is power of two? For n=??? M=2n-1. If M exactly power of two? e.g., n=(2^k+1)/2 not integer? M odd, cannot power of two. Ff > M, so pads. Good.
Memory: `weights` length M, rfft length Ff/2+1.

For `fast_convolution(x)`: `A*A` may produce tiny imaginary? irfft of A*A (complex) with non-Hermitian? Wait A*A (not conjugate) for real a's zero-padded DFT. Is A*A Hermitian? If A has Hermitian symmetry, A[-k]=conj(A[k]). Then (A^2)[-k] = A[-k]^2 = conj(A[k])^2 = conj(A[k]^2). Yes Hermitian. So irfft works. Due to numerical, okay.

`p_lbfgs_objective`:
Inputs:
- x: P[1:] length m = F//2 (for rfft length F/2+1, indices 1..F/2 inclusive; length F/2). If F=2, length? n≥1; F maybe 1 for n=1; m=0. Handle small.
- phase: complex length m+1.

- beta_y
- coeff

Implementation:
```
def p_lbfgs_objective(x, phase, F, M, n, beta_y, coeff, neg_gamma):
    m = len(x)
    P = np.empty(m+1)
    P[0]=1.0
    P[1:]=x
    P = np.clip(P,0.0,1.0)
    q = np.fft.irfft(P * phase, F)[:M]
    C = float(np.max(q))
    if C <= 1e-30 or not finite: ...
    scaled = beta_y*(q-C)/C; clip; exps; z; L = C + C log z/beta_y
    Fval = coeff*L
    weights = exps/z
    # gradient of L wrt P
    wfull = np.zeros(F)
    wfull[:M] = weights
    W = np.fft.rfft(wfull, F)
    grad = np.zeros(m+1)
    # m>=0
    if m==0:
       # only DC, grad irrelevant
    else:
       # factor 2/F for m=1..F//2-1; Nyquist factor 1/F if F even (always)
       idx = slice(1, m+1)
       g1 = (2.0/F) * np.real(phase[idx] * np.conj(W[idx]))
       grad[idx] = g1
       ny = F//2
       if ny <= m:
          grad[ny] *= 0.5
       # DC not optimized
    grad_out = coeff * C * grad[1:]
    # negative penalty
    if neg_gamma > 0:
       y = -q
       Cn = float(np.max(y))
       if Cn > 0.05*C: # only if meaningful
          scaledn = beta_y*(y-Cn)/Cn; clip; expsn; zn; Ln = Cn + Cn log zn/beta_y
          Fval += neg_gamma * Ln
          wfulln = zeros(F); wfulln[:M]=expsn/zn
          Wn = rfft(wfulln,F)
          g1n = (2.0/F)*real(phase[idx]*conj(Wn[idx])); grad[ny if]*=0.5
          # derivative of max(-q) wrt P = - grad_q, so subtract
          grad_out -= neg_gamma * Cn * g1n[1:]? Wait grad[1:] computed for y? We computed G_y = derivative of max(y) wrt P = sum wneg ∂(-q)/∂P = - sum wneg ∂q/∂P. Our formula with weights on y: derivative of y=-q wrt P = - derivative q. If we use same formula for y peaks, it already gives derivative of y? The formula `grad = Cn * sum weights * ∂q/∂P` if weights correspond to q positive peak. For y=-q, ∂y/∂P = -∂q/∂P. So we can compute base q-derivative H = Cn * sum weights ∂q/∂P; then penalty grad = -H. So g_base = Cn * (2/F) real(...). grad_out -= neg_gamma * g_base[1:]. Fval add neg_gamma*Ln.
    return Fval, grad_out
```
But for neg penalty, if we use weights on y, the derivative of y with respect to P is -(2/F) Re(...). We can compute with same `g_base` using phase and Wn but sign negative. So `grad_out -= neg_gamma * Cn * q_grad_component[1:]`. Need avoid recomputing base? We'll do:
```
qderiv_comp = (2/F)*real(phase[1:m+1]*conj(Wn[1:m+1])); if ny: qderiv_comp[ny-1]*=0.5
grad_out -= neg_gamma * Cn * qderiv_comp
```
Fval add.
Set neg_gamma default 0.0 to avoid complexity; maybe set 0.1.

But adding penalty changes objective not exactly score; final candidate score by compute_score. Good. We can use small `neg_gamma=0.0` initially. Could make it an arg to optimizer: use 0.2 for some runs.

`amp_phase_optimizer` details code:
```
def amp_phase_optimizer(a, label='', deadline=None, max_outer=3, beta_list=(50.0, 200.0, 1000.0), sub_maxiter=150, neg_gamma=0.0, keep_best_after=True):
    a = clip_normalize(a)
    n=len(a); F=fft_size(n); M=2*n-1; m=F//2
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_sequence(a)
    print label start score
    t0=time.monotonic()
    for outer in range(max_outer):
       if deadline and time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       A = np.fft.rfft(a,F)
       P = A.real*A.real + A.imag*A.imag
       # normalize P by P0? A[0]=sum a=1, but due to numerical, scale P by P[0]
       if P[0] <= 0: P += 1e-300
       P = P / P[0]
       P[0]=1.0
       P = np.clip(P,0.0,1.0)
       phi = np.angle(A)
       phi[0]=0.0
       if m < len(phi):
          # Nyquist enforce real
          imag = A[m].imag; real=A[m].real
          if abs(imag) < 1e-8*max(1.0, abs(real)):
              phi[m] = 0.0 if real >= 0 else np.pi
       phase = np.exp(1j*phi)
       x0 = P[1:].copy()
       bounds = [(0.0,1.0)]*m
       for beta_y in beta_list:
           if deadline and time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
           # Maybe scale maxiter by beta: lower beta can allow fewer?
           maxiter = sub_maxiter
           # Estimate time per iter? Just fixed.
           res = minimize(p_lbfgs_objective, x0, args=(phase, F, M, n, beta_y, 2.0*n, neg_gamma), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'maxfun':maxiter*20, 'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxls':30})
           x_opt = np.clip(res.x,0.0,1.0)
           P_opt = np.empty(m+1); P_opt[0]=1; P_opt[1:]=x_opt
           # try blends
           for t in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0):
              P_t = (1.0-t)*P + t*P_opt
              P_t[0]=1
              A_t = np.sqrt(np.maximum(P_t,0.0)) * np.exp(1j*phi)
              a_t = np.fft.irfft(A_t,F)[:n]
              # candidates: direct normalize of positive part
              cand = clip_normalize(a_t)
              sc = score_sequence(cand)
              if sc < best_sc: update
              # also blend cand with best_a
              for mix in (0.5,0.8):
                 cand2 = (1-mix)*best_a + mix*cand; cand2=clip_normalize(cand2); sc2=score...
              # maybe spectral_compress from a_t? not now
           x0 = x_opt
       # optional: run spectral_compress on best_a for a few iterations to alter phase
       a_scr = spectral_compress_fienup(best_a, n_iters=30, top_frac_init=0.1, top_frac_end=0.5, gamma=0.8)
       sc_song... update; if improved, continue; else break?
    return best_sc, best_a
```

Now, `spectral_compress_fienup` implementation top-level:
```
def spectral_compress_fienup(a, n_iters=100, top_frac_init=0.1, top_frac_end=0.5, gamma=0.8, soft_alpha=30.0):
    a=clip_normalize(a); n; F; M;
    best_sc=score(a); best_a=a.copy()
    for it in range(n_iters):
      if deadline passed? Optional arg deadline.
      P=|rfft(a,F)|^2
      top_frac=...
      thresh=np.quantile(P, 1-top_frac)
      scale=np.ones_like(P)
      above=P>thresh
      if above.any(): ratio=np.clip(thresh/P[above],0,10); scale[above]=np.sqrt(np.maximum(ratio,1e-12))*gamma
      A=np.fft.rfft(a,F); A*=scale
      a_new=np.fft.irfft(A,F)[:n]
      if soft_alpha>0: gate=1/(1+exp(-soft_alpha*a_new)); a_new=a_new*gate
      a_new=clip_normalize(np.maximum(a_new,0))
      sc=score(a_new); update; a=a_new?
    return best
```
Should we update a to best or latest? Previous updates a to latest; keep track best. Use latest to continue. If score NaN break.

```
def project_nonnegative_sum(a):
    a = np.asarray(a)
    S = a.sum()
    if S <=0: return ones
    # variant scale: max(a,0)/summax
    # variant add: pos mask >0; if pos count>0 and neg mass not huge, add neg to positives; if result negative? no.
    v1 = np.maximum(a,0); v1sum=v1.sum(); if v1sum>0 v1=v1/v1sum
    neg = -np.minimum(a,0).sum()
    v2 = np.maximum(a,0)
    pos = v2 > 0
    if pos.any(): v2[pos] += neg/pos.count(); v2=v2/v2.sum()
    # maybe gate variant
```

def additive_nonnegative(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); S=a.sum(); if S<=0 uniform
   a = a / S  # maybe normalize first?
   neg = -np.minimum(a,0).sum()

   mask=b>0
   if neg > 0.5: # too much, just scale
      return b/b.sum()
   if mask.sum()>0:
      b[mask] += neg / mask.sum()
   return b / b.sum()

```
def amp_phase_optimizer(a, label='', deadline=None, max_outer=3, sub_maxiter=200, beta_targets=(300.0,1000.0,3000.0), neg_gamma=0.0):
    a = clip_normalize(a)
    n=len(a)
    F=fft_size(n); M=2*n-1; m=F//2
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_sequence(a)
    print(...)
    for outer in range(max_outer):
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       A = np.fft.rfft(a, F)
       P = A.real*A.real + A.imag*A.imag
       if P[0] <= 1e-300:
          P = np.zeros(m+1); P[0]=1
       else:
          P = P / P[0]
       P[0] = 1.0
       # P may exceed 1 due to numeric/invalid? clip
       P = np.clip(P, 0.0, 1.0)
       C0 = float(np.max(np.fft.irfft(P*np.exp(1j*np.angle(A)), F)[:M])) # or score C from a? Use a C: fast_conv a
       C0 = score? Better compute from a: q=fast_convolution(a); C0=q.max()
       phi = np.angle(A); phi[0]=0; enforce nyq
       phase = np.exp(1j*phi)
       x0 = P[1:]
       bounds = [(0.0,1.0)]*m
       for target in beta_targets:
          if deadline and time.monotonic() > deadline-5: break
          beta_eff = min(1e8, max(1e5, target / max(C0,1e-12)))
          maxiter = sub_maxiter
          if F > 32768: maxiter=int(maxiter*0.6); if F>65536 *0.3
          res = minimize(p_lbfgs_objective, x0, args=(phase,F,M,n,beta_eff,2.0*n, neg_gamma), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxiter*12,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':20})
          if res.x finite:
             x_opt=np.clip(res.x,0,1)
          else: x_opt=x0
          # candidate P blends
          for t in (0.3,0.6,1.0):
             P_t = (1.0-t)*P + t*np.concatenate(([1],x_opt))? But concatenating each time. We can create P_opt once.
             A_t = sqrt(P_t)*exp(1j phi)
             a_t = irfft(A_t,F)[:n]
             for proj in [clip_normalize, additive_nonnegative, gate_nonneg(20)]:
               cand=proj(a_t); sc=score; update
             if t==1.0:
                for mix in (0.5,0.8): cand=(1-mix)*best_a + mix*cand_scale; update
           x0 = np.clip(x_opt,0,1)
       # fienup warm
       if deadline and time < deadline-30:
          a_f = spectral_compress_fienup(best_a, n_iters=50, top_frac_init=0.1, top_frac_end=0.4, gamma=0.8, soft_alpha=20, deadline=deadline)
          update if better
       # if best_a improved little? continue anyway
    return best_sc,best_a
```

def a_lbfgs_refine(a, deadline, beta_targets=(300,1500,6000), maxiter=200):

    # spectral compress if time
  return best

Let's construct `build_initializations` returning list maybe up to max. Use `rng` passed. No nested.
```
def build_initializations(n, parent, rng, max_variants=6):
    inits=[]
    i=np.arange(n)
    if parent is not None:
       p=np.asarray(parent,float).ravel()
       if p.size>1:
          if p.size==n: inits.append(p)
          else: inits.append(np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), np.linspace(0,1,p.size), p))
    inits.append(np.ones(n))
    x=(i+0.5)/n
    inits.append(0.5+0.5*np.sin(np.pi*x))
    inits.append(0.5+0.4*np.cos(2*np.pi*(x-0.5)))
    # low freq random
    z=np.ones(n)
    for m in range(1,6): ... z += ...
    z=np.maximum(z,0.05); inits.append(z)
    # antisym with random
    z=np.ones(n); c=(n-1)/2; for m... sin; z=np.maximum(z,0.05)
    # smoothed random

Now, p_objective code:
```
def p_lbfgs_objective(x, phase, F, M, n, beta_eff, coeff, neg_gamma):
    m = len(x)
    P = np.empty(m+1, dtype=np.float64)
    P[0]=1.0
    if m: P[1:]=x
    np.clip(P,0.0,1.0,out=P)
    q = np.fft.irfft(P * phase, F)[:M]
    if not finite q: return ...
    C = float(np.max(q))
    if C <= 1e-300: return 1e100, zeros
    scaled = beta_eff * (q - C)
    scaled = np.clip(scaled, -745.0, 0.0)
    exps = np.exp(scaled)
    z = float(np.sum(exps))
    if z <= 0: exps[0]=1; z=1
    lnz = np.log(z)
    Fval = coeff * (C + lnz / beta_eff)
    weights = exps / z
    grad = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if m:
       W = np.fft.rfft(weights, F)
       g = (2.0/F) * np.real(phase[1:m+1] * np.conj(W[1:m+1]))
       ni = F//2
       if ni <= m:
          g[ni-1] *= 0.5
       grad = coeff * g
       if neg_gamma and neg_gamma>0: ...
    if not np.isfinite(Fval): return 1e100, zeros
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return float(Fval), grad
```
But note if `m=0`, grad empty. For small n not. `phase[1:m+1]` length m. If F//2 may equal m (since phase length m+1). Good.
If `beta_eff` very large and `q` contains huge negative, `scaled` maybe beta*(q-C) < -745, exp=0. `z` at least 1 due to max term (where scaled=0). Good.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    total=float(budget_s)
    hard_end=t_start+total-20
    deadline=t_start+total-180  # reserve final
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=None
    try:
       p=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if p.size >= 8:
           p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
           if p.sum()>0:
               parent=clip_normalize(p)
    except Exception:
       parent=None
    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    if parent is not None:
       sc=score_sequence(parent); if finite: best
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120:
           a_f=spectral_compress_fienup(parent, n_iters=150, top_frac_init=0.1, top_frac_end=0.45, gamma=0.75, soft_alpha=25.0, deadline=deadline-60)
           sc=score; update
    if best_a is None:
       best_a=clip_normalize(np.ones(4096)); best_sc=score
    # lengths
    ordered=[]
    if parent is not None:
       ordered.append(len(parent))
    for l in (8192, 12288, 16384, 24576):
       if l not in ordered:
          ordered.append(l)
    # if parent length > 24576? still include parent and maybe 16384
    if len(ordered)>5: ordered=ordered[:5]
    # maybe remove lengths too large relative time? if estimated cost.
    print...
    for length_idx,n in enumerate(ordered):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60: break
        # determine number inits
        F=fft_size(n)
        if F <= 16384: max_var=4; per_time=240
        elif F <=32768: max_var=3; per_time=200
        else: max_var=2; per_time=140
        inits=build_initializations(n,parent,rng,max_variants=max_var)
        for init in inits:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60: break
            now=time.monotonic()
            if now + per_time > hard_end: break
            subdeadline=min(deadline, now + per_time)
            # quick amp
            a_opt, sc_opt = amp_phase_optimizer(init, label=f'amp n={n}', deadline=subdeadline, max_outer=2, sub_maxiter=150 if F>32768 else 250, beta_targets=(300,800))
            if sc_opt < best_sc:
                 best_sc=sc_opt; best_a=a_opt.copy(); print
                 # maybe quick final? later
            # If init parent length and parent score was best? skip? okay.
    # Final polish on best_a
    polish_deadline=hard_end-30
    while time.monotonic() < polish_deadline - 200:
        n=len(best_a)
        F=fft_size(n)
        maxiter=300 if F<=32768 else 150
        a_opt, sc_opt=a_lbfgs_refine(best_a, deadline=polish_deadline, beta_targets=(300,1200,4000), maxiter=maxiter)
        if sc_opt < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc=sc_opt; best_a=a_opt; print
        else:
            # try spectral compress
            a_f=spectral_compress_fienup(best_a, n_iters=100, top_frac_init=0.08, top_frac_end=0.5, gamma=0.7, soft_alpha=20, deadline=polish_deadline)
            sc=score(a_f); if improved update else break
    # one final amp phase if time
    if time.monotonic() < hard_end-120:
       a_opt, sc_opt=amp_phase_optimizer(best_a, label='final amp', deadline=hard_end-60, max_outer=2, sub_maxiter=150, beta_targets=(800,2500))
       update
    if time.monotonic() < hard_end-60:
       a_lbfgs_refine(best_a, deadline=hard_end-20, beta_targets=(5000,), maxiter=100)
    # output
    out=[float(max(0.0,min(1000.0,x))) for x in best_a]
    if sum(out)<0.01: out=[0.5]*64
    try: verified=evaluate_sequence(out)
    except: verified=np.inf
    print
    return out
```

```
def spectral_compress_fienup(a, n_iters=100, ..., deadline=None):
  a=clip_normalize(a); n; F; best...
  for it:
    if deadline and time.monotonic()>deadline: break
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,F)
    P=A.real*A.real + A.imag*A.imag
    top_frac=...
    thresh=float(np.quantile(P, 1.0-top_frac))
    scale=np.ones_like(P)
    above=P>thresh
    if above.any():
       ratio=thresh/P[above]
       scale[above]=np.sqrt(np.maximum(ratio,1e-30))*gamma
    A2=A*scale
    a_new=np.fft.irfft(A2,F)[:n]
    if soft_alpha>0: ...
    a_new=clip_normalize(np.maximum(a_new,0))
    sc=score_sequence(a_new)
    if sc<best_sc: update
    a=a_new
  return best
```

`additive_nonnegative`:
```
def additive_nonnegative(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S<=0 or not finite: return clip_normalize(np.ones_like(a))
    a=a/S
    neg=-float(np.minimum(a,0).sum())
    b=np.maximum(a,0)
    bs=b.sum()
    if bs <= 1e-15: return clip_normalize(b)
    if neg > 0.5:  # too much negative, fallback scale
        return b/bs
    mask=b>0
    if mask.any():
        b[mask] += neg / float(mask.sum())
    return b / b.sum()
```
If neg small, good.

`clip_normalize`:
```
def clip_normalize(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   S=a.sum()
   if S<=1e-15:
       n=a.size
       if n==0: return np.ones(1)
       return np.ones(n)/n
   return a/S
```
Maybe cap at 1.0? If a has values >1 due to additive, sum1 but max>1 possible? Nonnegative sum1 implies max≤1 if no negatives. additive after sum normalization nonneg => max≤1. scaling max≤1. Good. clip to 1.0 not necessary. But initial inits may values O(1), normalize. Use cap 1000 for safety.

Let's code `fft_size`:
```
def fft_size(n):
    m = 2*n - 1
    return 1 << (max(1, m-1).bit_length())
```
If m=1, max(1,0)=1 bit_length1 ->2? Wait 1<<(1).bit_length? Python: int bit_length method: (m - 1).bit_length() for m=1 =>0.bit_length()=0 ->1. But if m=0? n=0 no. Use:

return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```
For m=1 ->1. For m=2 -> (1).bit_length=1 ->2. Good. If m=0, error; n>=1.

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The implementation uses:
- a direct normalized LSE-smoothed least-squares/refinement objective,
- an amplitude-spectrum (phase-preserving) L-BFGS sub-problem that directly
  shapes the auto-convolution,
- non-negative reconstruction projections (scaling, additive repair, sigmoid gate),
- classic spectral peak compression as a warm-start / polish,
- multiple lengths and smooth/structured initializations.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def fft_size(n: int) -> int:
    m = 2 * int(n) - 1
    if m < 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def clip_normalize(a) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def fast_convolution(a) -> np.ndarray:
    """Full linear convolution a * a using zero-padded FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    F = fft_size(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, F)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, F)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def score_sequence(a) -> float:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    C = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(C):
        return np.inf
    if C < 0.0 and C > -1e-14:
        C = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * C / (S * S))


def choose_beta_from_C(C: float, target_mul: float,
                       min_beta: float = 1e5, max_beta: float = 1e8) -> float:
    if not np.isfinite(C) or C <= 1e-300:
        return float(min_beta)
    beta = target_mul / max(C, 1e-300)
    return float(min(max_beta, max(min_beta, beta)))


def spectral_compress_fienup(a,
                             n_iters: int = 100,
                             top_frac_init: float = 0.10,
                             top_frac_end: float = 0.45,
                             gamma: float = 0.75,
                             soft_alpha: float = 25.0,
                             deadline: float = None):
    """
    Warm-start / polish by repeatedly compressing large |FFT(a)|^2 bins,
    inverse transforming, projecting to non-negativity, and normalizing.
    """
    a = clip_normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    F = fft_size(n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_sequence(a)

    for it in range(n_iters):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        A = np.fft.rfft(a, F)
        P = A.real * A.real + A.imag * A.imag

        progress = it / max(1, n_iters)
        top_frac = top_frac_init + (top_frac_end - top_frac_init) * progress
        if top_frac <= 0.0:
            continue
        if top_frac >= 1.0:
            top_frac = 0.999999

        thresh = float(np.quantile(P, 1.0 - top_frac))
        scale = np.ones_like(P)
        above = P > thresh
        if above.any():
            ratio = thresh / np.maximum(P[above], 1e-300)
            ratio = np.clip(ratio, 1e-12, 1e12)
            scale[above] = np.sqrt(ratio) * gamma

        A_new = A * scale
        a_new = np.fft.irfft(A_new, F)[:n]

        if soft_alpha > 0:
            z = np.clip(soft_alpha * a_new, -50.0, 50.0)
            gate = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-z))
            a_new = a_new * gate

        a_new = clip_normalize(np.maximum(a_new, 0.0))
        sc = score_sequence(a_new)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a_new.copy()
        a = a_new

    return best_sc, best_a


def additive_nonnegative(a) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Normalize, then replace negative mass by adding it to positive samples.
    Falls back to scaling if negative mass is too large.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size

    a = a / S
    neg = -float(np.sum(np.minimum(a, 0.0)))
    b = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    bs = float(np.sum(b))
    if bs <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size

    if neg <= 0.0 or neg > 0.5:
        return b / bs

    mask = b > 1e-300
    if mask.any():
        b[mask] += neg / float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
    return b / float(np.sum(b))


def gate_nonnegative(a, alpha: float = 25.0) -> np.ndarray:
    """Soft non-negativity projection via a sigmoid gate."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    z = np.clip(alpha * a, -50.0, 50.0)
    gate = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-z))
    return clip_normalize(a * gate)


def p_lbfgs_objective(x, phase, F, M, n, beta_eff, coeff, neg_gamma):
    """
    Objective and gradient for optimizing P = |A|^2 with fixed phase of A^2.
    x holds P[1:]. P[0] is fixed to 1.
    """
    m = int(len(x))
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)

    P = np.empty(m + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    P[0] = 1.0
    if m:
        P[1:] = x
    np.clip(P, 0.0, 1.0, out=P)

    q = np.fft.irfft(P * phase, F)[:M]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

    C = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(C) or C <= 1e-300:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

    scaled = beta_eff * (q - C)
    scaled = np.clip(scaled, -745.0, 0.0)
    exps = np.exp(scaled)
    zsum = float(np.sum(exps))
    if zsum <= 1e-300:
        exps[0] = 1.0
        zsum = 1.0

    L = C + float(np.log(zsum)) / beta_eff
    Fval = coeff * L

    grad = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if m:
        weights = exps / zsum
        W = np.fft.rfft(weights, F)
        g = (2.0 / F) * np.real(phase[1:m + 1] * np.conj(W[1:m + 1]))
        ny = F // 2
        if ny <= m:
            g[ny - 1] *= 0.5
        grad = coeff * g

    if neg_gamma is not None and neg_gamma > 0.0:
        y = -q
        Cn = float(np.max(y))
        if np.isfinite(Cn) and Cn > 1e-14 * max(C, 1.0):
            scaledn = beta_eff * (y - Cn)
            scaledn = np.clip(scaledn, -745.0, 0.0)
            expsn = np.exp(scaledn)
            zn = float(np.sum(expsn))
            if zn <= 1e-300:
                expsn[0] = 1.0
                zn = 1.0
            Ln = Cn + float(np.log(zn)) / beta_eff
            Fval += coeff * neg_gamma * Ln

            weightsn = expsn / zn
            Wn = np.fft.rfft(weightsn, F)
            gn = (2.0 / F) * np.real(phase[1:m + 1] * np.conj(Wn[1:m + 1]))
            if ny <= m:
                gn[ny - 1] *= 0.5
            grad -= coeff * neg_gamma * gn

    if not np.isfinite(Fval):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return float(Fval), grad


def amp_phase_optimizer(a,
                        label: str = "",
                        deadline: float = None,
                        max_outer: int = 3,
                        sub_maxiter: int = 200,
                        beta_targets=(300.0, 1000.0, 3000.0),
                        neg_gamma: float = 0.0):
    """
    Alternate:
      1) from a non-negative a, get A, P=|A|^2 and phase of A^2,
      2) optimize P by L-BFGS to flatten IFFT(P * phase),
      3) reconstruct A and project back to non-negative sequences.
    """
    a = clip_normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    F = fft_size(n)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    m = F // 2

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_sequence(a)
    print(f"  [amp {label}] start n={n} score={best_sc:.8f}")

    if m <= 0:
        return best_sc, best_a

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * m

    for outer in range(max_outer):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        old_sc = best_sc

        for target in beta_targets:
            if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline - 20:
                break

            a = best_a
            A = np.fft.rfft(a, F)
            ny = F // 2
            if ny < len(A):
                A = A.copy()
                A[ny] = np.complex128(A[ny].real, 0.0)

            AA = A * A
            q0 = np.fft.irfft(AA, F)[:M]
            C0 = float(np.max(q0)) if q0.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(C0) or C0 <= 1e-300:
                C0 = 1e-4

            P = A.real * A.real + A.imag * A.imag
            if P[0] <= 1e-300:
                P = np.zeros(m + 1, dtype=np.float64)
                P[0] = 1.0
            else:
                P = P / P[0]
            P[0] = 1.0
            np.clip(P, 0.0, 1.0, out=P)

            phi = np.angle(A)
            phi[0] = 0.0
            if ny < len(phi):
                phi[ny] = 0.0 if A[ny].real >= 0.0 else np.pi

            phase = np.exp(1j * phi)
            x0 = P[1:].copy()

            rem = 3600.0
            if deadline is not None:
                rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
                if rem < 30.0:
                    break

            maxiter_use = int(sub_maxiter * min(1.0, rem / 120.0))
            maxiter_use = max(25, min(sub_maxiter, maxiter_use))

            beta_eff = choose_beta_from_C(C0, target)
            coeff = 2.0 * float(n)

            try:
                res = minimize(
                    p_lbfgs_objective,
                    x0.copy(),
                    args=(phase, F, M, n, beta_eff, coeff, neg_gamma),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    jac=True,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options={
                        "maxiter": maxiter_use,
                        "maxfun": max(200, maxiter_use * 10),
                        "ftol": 1e-14,
                        "gtol": 1e-10,
                        "maxls": 25,
                    }
                )
                x_opt = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_opt)):
                    x_opt = x0
            except Exception:
                x_opt = x0

            x_opt = np.where(np.isfinite(x_opt), x_opt, 0.0)
            np.clip(x_opt, 0.0, 1.0, out=x_opt)

            P_opt = np.empty(m + 1, dtype=np.float64)
            P_opt[0] = 1.0
            P_opt[1:] = x_opt

            for t in (0.30, 0.65, 1.00):
                P_t = (1.0 - t) * P + t * P_opt
                P_t[0] = 1.0
                np.clip(P_t, 0.0, 1.0, out=P_t)

                A_t = np.sqrt(P_t) * np.exp(1j * phi)
                a_t = np.fft.irfft(A_t, F)[:n]

                cand1 = clip_normalize(a_t)
                sc = score_sequence(cand1)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = cand1.copy()
                    print(f"    [amp {label}] outer={outer} beta={target:g} t={t:.2f} "
                          f"scale score={sc:.8f}")

                cand2 = additive_nonnegative(a_t)
                sc = score_sequence(cand2)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = cand2.copy()
                    print(f"    [amp {label}] outer={outer} beta={target:g} t={t:.2f} "
                          f"add score={sc:.8f}")

                cand3 = gate_nonnegative(a_t, 25.0)
                sc = score_sequence(cand3)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = cand3.copy()
                    print(f"    [amp {label}] outer={outer} beta={target:g} t={t:.2f} "
                          f"gate score={sc:.8f}")

                if t >= 0.99:
                    for mix in (0.35, 0.70):
                        candb = (1.0 - mix) * best_a + mix * cand1
                        candb = clip_normalize(candb)
                        sc = score_sequence(candb)
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                            best_sc = sc
                            best_a = candb.copy()
                            print(f"    [amp {label}] outer={outer} beta={target:g} "
                                  f"mix={mix:.2f} score={sc:.8f}")

        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline - 30:
            break

        if best_sc == old_sc:
            # try one spectral repair; if it does not help, stop the outer loop
            fs, af = spectral_compress_fienup(
                best_a,
                n_iters=35,
                top_frac_init=0.08,
                top_frac_end=0.45,
                gamma=0.72,
                soft_alpha=20.0,
                deadline=(deadline - 18) if deadline is not None else None
            )
            if np.isfinite(fs) and fs < best_sc:
                best_sc = fs
                best_a = af.copy()
            else:
                break
        else:
            # small spectral refresh to change phase after a good amplitude move
            fs, af = spectral_compress_fienup(
                best_a,
                n_iters=25,
                top_frac_init=0.10,
                top_frac_end=0.35,
                gamma=0.85,
                soft_alpha=30.0,
                deadline=(deadline - 30) if deadline is not None else None
            )
            if np.isfinite(fs) and fs < best_sc:
                best_sc = fs
                best_a = af.copy()

    print(f"  [amp {label}] best n={n} score={best_sc:.8f}")
    return best_sc, best_a


def a_lbfgs_objective(x, beta_eff):
    """
    Objective and gradient for minimizing
        f(z) = 2n * LSE(conv(z / sum z))
    which is scale invariant. This is a smoothed approximation of the target.
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(x))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    y = x / S
    c = fast_convolution(y)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    C = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(C) or C <= 1e-300:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    scaled = beta_eff * (c - C)
    scaled = np.clip(scaled, -745.0, 0.0)
    exps = np.exp(scaled)
    zsum = float(np.sum(exps))
    if zsum <= 1e-300:
        exps[0] = 1.0
        zsum = 1.0

    L = C + float(np.log(zsum)) / beta_eff
    Fval = 2.0 * n * L

    weights = exps / zsum
    Ff = fft_size(n)
    W = np.fft.rfft(weights, Ff)
    A = np.fft.rfft(y, Ff)
    r = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(A), Ff)[:n]

    # grad of L wrt y
    grad_L = 2.0 * r
    H_y = 2.0 * n * grad_L

    # Correct chain-rule gradient for f(x) = H(x / sum x).
    weighted = float(np.sum(y * H_y))
    grad_x = (H_y - y * weighted) / S

    if not np.isfinite(Fval):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    grad_x = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_x), grad_x, 0.0)
    return float(Fval), grad_x


def a_lbfgs_refine(a,
                   deadline: float = None,
                   beta_targets=(500.0, 2000.0, 8000.0),
                   maxiter: int = 200):
    """
    Direct refinement on the non-negative time-domain variables using L-BFGS-B
    and smoothed LSE objective.
    """
    a = clip_normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_sequence(a)

    if n == 0:
        return best_sc, best_a

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

    for target in beta_targets:
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline - 60:
            break

        y = best_a / max(float(np.sum(best_a)), 1e-15)
        c = fast_convolution(y)
        C0 = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 1e-4
        if not np.isfinite(C0) or C0 <= 1e-300:
            C0 = 1e-4

        beta_eff = choose_beta_from_C(C0, target)

        rem = 1200.0
        if deadline is not None:
            rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
            if rem < 90:
                break

        maxiter_use = int(maxiter * min(1.0, rem / 300.0))
        maxiter_use = max(25, min(maxiter, maxiter_use))

        try:
            res = minimize(
                a_lbfgs_objective,
                best_a.copy(),
                args=(beta_eff,),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter_use,
                    "maxfun": max(200, maxiter_use * 15),
                    "ftol": 1e-14,
                    "gtol": 1e-10,
                    "maxls": 30,
                }
            )
            cand = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
                cand = best_a
        except Exception:
            cand = best_a

        cand = clip_normalize(cand)
        sc = score_sequence(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = cand.copy()
            print(f"  [a-lbfgs] target={target:g} n={n} score={sc:.8f}")
        else:
            # If L-BFGS did not improve, stop later targets to save time.
            break

    return best_sc, best_a


def build_initializations(n: int, parent, rng, max_variants: int = 4):
    """Diverse smooth initializations for a given length."""
    inits = []
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if parent is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size >= 8 and np.isfinite(p).any():
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size == n:
                inits.append(p.copy())
            else:
                xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
                xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
                inits.append(np.interp(xn, xo, p).copy())

    # Uniform.
    inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    # Smooth deterministic shapes.
    x = (i + 0.5) / n
    inits.append(0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(np.pi * x))
    inits.append(np.maximum(0.45 + 0.45 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * (x - 0.5)), 0.02))

    # Smooth random low-frequency waves.
    z = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, 6):
        a1 = rng.uniform(-0.7, 0.7)
        b1 = rng.uniform(-0.7, 0.7)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        z += a1 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph)
        z += b1 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph)
    z = np.maximum(z, 0.05)
    inits.append(z)

    # Anti-symmetric about the center in the deviation, helpful for center lags.
    z = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c = (n - 1) * 0.5
    xx = (i - c) / max(1, n)
    for m in range(1, 5):
        amp = rng.uniform(-0.65, 0.65)
        z += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * xx)
    z = np.maximum(z, 0.05)
    inits.append(z)

    # Smoothed random perturbation.
    z = np.clip(0.75 + 0.35 * rng.standard_normal(n), 0.02, 2.0)
    kern = np.ones(5, dtype=np.float64) / 5.0
    z = np.convolve(z, kern, mode="same")
    z = np.maximum(z, 0.02)
    inits.append(z)

    # Asymmetric ramp.
    inits.append(0.25 + 0.75 * x)

    out = []
    for arr in inits:
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size != n:
            continue
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.02, 10.0)
        s = float(np.sum(arr))
        if s > 1e-15:
            out.append(arr / s)
        if len(out) >= int(max_variants):
            break
    if not out:
        out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n)
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed: int = 42, budget_s: float = 2400.0):
    """
    Main search entry point. Returns the best non-negative sequence found
    before the time budget expires.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)
    hard_end = t_start + max(120.0, total - 20.0)
    global_deadline = t_start + max(120.0, total - 240.0)  # reserve for final polish
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Load already-found construction if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size >= 8 and np.isfinite(p).any():
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            S = float(np.sum(p))
            if S > 1e-12:
                parent = p / S
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    if parent is not None:
        parent = clip_normalize(parent)
        sc = score_sequence(parent)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = parent.copy()
            print(f"[init] parent n={parent.size} score={best_sc:.8f}")

        if time.monotonic() < global_deadline - 240:
            fs, af = spectral_compress_fienup(
                parent,
                n_iters=180,
                top_frac_init=0.08,
                top_frac_end=0.42,
                gamma=0.75,
                soft_alpha=25.0,
                deadline=global_deadline - 120
            )
            if np.isfinite(fs) and fs < best_sc:
                best_sc = fs
                best_a = af.copy()
                print(f"[parent fienup] improved score={best_sc:.8f}")

    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_a = clip_normalize(np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64))
        best_sc = score_sequence(best_a)
        print(f"[init] fallback uniform score={best_sc:.8f}")

    # Candidate lengths: parent first, then a few larger smooth lengths.
    ordered_lengths = []
    if parent is not None:
        ordered_lengths.append(int(parent.size))
    for L in (8192, 12288, 16384):
        if L not in ordered_lengths:
            ordered_lengths.append(L)
    ordered_lengths = [L for L in ordered_lengths if 64 <= L <= 20000][:4]

    print(f"[search] lengths: {ordered_lengths}, deadline reserve=240s")

    for n in ordered_lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > global_deadline - 120:
            break

        F = fft_size(n)
        if F <= 16384:
            max_variants = 4
            per_candidate = 220.0
            amp_outer = 3
            amp_sub_iter = 220
            beta_targets = (400.0, 1200.0, 3000.0)
        elif F <= 32768:
            max_variants = 3
            per_candidate = 180.0
            amp_outer = 2
            amp_sub_iter = 150
            beta_targets = (400.0, 1200.0, 2500.0)
        else:
            max_variants = 2
            per_candidate = 120.0
            amp_outer = 2
            amp_sub_iter = 90
            beta_targets = (400.0, 1200.0)

        inits = build_initializations(n, parent, rng, max_variants=max_variants)
        for idx, init in enumerate(inits):
            if time.monotonic() > global_deadline - 120:
                break
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now + per_candidate > hard_end:
                break
            subdeadline = min(global_deadline, now + per_candidate)

            try:
                sc, aa = amp_phase_optimizer(
                    init,
                    label=f"n={n} init={idx}",
                    deadline=subdeadline,
                    max_outer=amp_outer,
                    sub_maxiter=amp_sub_iter,
                    beta_targets=beta_targets,
                    neg_gamma=0.0 if F <= 16384 else 0.05
                )
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[search] amp_phase_optimizer failed for n={n} init={idx}: {e}")
                continue

            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = aa.copy()
                print(f"[search] new global best n={aa.size} score={best_sc:.8f}")

        # After finishing a length, if we improved global best, one direct polish.
        if time.monotonic() < global_deadline - 120:
            nbest = int(best_a.size)
            Fb = fft_size(nbest)
            mi = 180 if Fb <= 32768 else 90
            try:
                sc2, aa2 = a_lbfgs_refine(
                    best_a,
                    deadline=global_deadline - 100,
                    beta_targets=(800.0, 3000.0),
                    maxiter=mi
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = aa2.copy()
                    print(f"[search] direct polish improved n={nbest} score={best_sc:.8f}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[search] polish failed: {e}")

    # Final polish stage.
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = clip_normalize(np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64))
        best_sc = score_sequence(best_a)

    final_deadline = hard_end - 20.0
    print(f"[final polish] current best={best_sc:.8f}, n={int(best_a.size)}")

    # Direct L-BFGS with increasingly exact LSE.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 180:
        nbest = int(best_a.size)
        Fb = fft_size(nbest)
        mi = 350 if Fb <= 32768 else 180
        try:
            sc, aa = a_lbfgs_refine(
                best_a,
                deadline=final_deadline - 90,
                beta_targets=(1000.0, 4000.0, 10000.0),
                maxiter=mi
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = aa.copy()
                print(f"[final] a-lbfgs improved score={best_sc:.8f}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[final] a-lbfgs failed: {e}")

    # Amplitude/phase round on the best sequence if time remains.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 240:
        nbest = int(best_a.size)
        Fb = fft_size(nbest)
        if Fb <= 32768:
            mi = 220
            outer = 3
        else:
            mi = 110
            outer = 2
        try:
            sc, aa = amp_phase_optimizer(
                best_a,
                label="final-amp",
                deadline=final_deadline - 120,
                max_outer=outer,
                sub_maxiter=mi,
                beta_targets=(1500.0, 5000.0, 12000.0),
                neg_gamma=0.0
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = aa.copy()
                print(f"[final] amp improved score={best_sc:.8f}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[final] amp failed: {e}")

    # One short spectral compression, then a very short L-BFGS finish.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 120:
        try:
            sc, aa = spectral_compress_fienup(
                best_a,
                n_iters=80,
                top_frac_init=0.06,
                top_frac_end=0.45,
                gamma=0.68,
                soft_alpha=20.0,
                deadline=final_deadline - 70
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = aa.copy()
                print(f"[final] fienup improved score={best_sc:.8f}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[final] fienup failed: {e}")

    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 45:
        nbest = int(best_a.size)
        Fb = fft_size(nbest)
        mi = 140 if Fb <= 32768 else 60
        try:
            sc, aa = a_lbfgs_refine(
                best_a,
                deadline=final_deadline - 10,
                beta_targets=(8000.0,),
                maxiter=mi
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = aa.copy()
                print(f"[final] short a-lbfgs improved score={best_sc:.8f}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[final] short a-lbfgs failed: {e}")

    # Sanitize output.
    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]
    if len(out) == 0 or sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float("inf")

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal={best_sc:.8f}, verified={verified:.8f}, len={len(out)}, "
          f"time={elapsed:.1f}s / {total:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 70)

    return out
```
